Milestone 9.1 (PR #26) merged into `main` as `fe1a381`; this branch merged it in `99f4272` (a merge commit, clean). **Re-checked on main `fe1a381`: no change.** `git diff --stat 7c05601 fe1a381` touches only `crates/cli/tests/run_e2e_orch/{common,steer}.rs` (`f06848c`, the steer end-to-end fix), so every check-1 name, shape and `file:line` above, every check-6 count, and the bases (`PROTO_VERSION = 12` at `proto/src/lib.rs:66`, `HISTORY_VERSION = 3` at `history.rs:20`, read from `fe1a381` itself) are as recorded. The header's guard now holds: 9.1 is on `main`, and 9.2's protocol is 13 (14 since the merge with 9.0.6; see that heading).

#### Controller rulings on M9.2.1's findings (2026-10-01)

The controller accepted M9.2.1 (`dcd687e`) after checking the fixtures (no email, token or real login). Each ruling amends the decision it names; where a decision's text and a ruling disagree, the ruling wins.

- **R-2 (finding 1, the watermark; decisions 7, 14, 23, 29).** `THREADS_QUERY` is extended into one GraphQL read (`gh api graphql`, a query only, never a mutation). It reads the review threads, `reviews` and the issue `comments`, each with `fullDatabaseId`. Watermarks compare `fullDatabaseId` as a `u64` parsed from its string; a parse failure is a host error. `databaseId` is never used. `gh pr view` stays the source of state, checks and mergeability. The allow-list (decision 7) and `FakeGh` (decision 14) accept exactly the extended query.
- **R-3 (finding 2, bots; decision 29).** The same query reads `author { __typename login }`. A bot is `__typename == "Bot"`; it is never inferred from the login text.
- **R-4 (finding 3, the merge method; decision 44).** After the base fetch, the merge commit's parents are counted locally with `git rev-list --parents -n 1 <oid>`, through the git queue with the usual flags (`run_git`: `--no-optional-locks`, scrubbed environment, deadline). Two or more parents is `merge`; one parent is `squash_or_rebase`, told apart as the decision does (against the stage's commits).
- **R-5 (finding 4, decision 27).** Pending: `QUEUED`, `IN_PROGRESS`, `PENDING`, `REQUESTED`, `WAITING`, `EXPECTED`. Red: a check run's `FAILURE`, `TIMED_OUT`, `CANCELLED`, `ACTION_REQUIRED`, `STARTUP_FAILURE` (the decision's list), and a status context's `ERROR` and `FAILURE`. Any unknown value counts as pending, never green.
- **R-6 (finding 5, decision 11; finding 6, decision 29).** `You are not logged into any accounts` joins the logged-out patterns (`HostError::Auth`). Write access is `role_name` ∈ {`admin`, `maintain`, `write`}; a custom role name, or none, is no write access, and the comment is ignored with its reason.
- **R-7 (finding 7, decision 33).** A base sync has its own emit path in `engine/delivery/sync.rs`, not 9.1's `propagate::start`, and works for `Single` runs. It is journaled either as its own op kind or as a `Propagate` with `PropagateSpec.from = 0`, which restore checks. Reconcile restores a lost base sync as a base sync (back into `base_sync_due`), never as a stage propagate. A test restarts the daemon in the middle of a base sync.
- **R-8 (finding 8, decision 37).** In `pr` mode, completion means every stage PR is merged on the host (closed and skipped stages per decision 37). `full::completion` and `full::holds_completion` are both bypassed: CI on the PR is the gate. `propagate::holds_completion` still applies (nothing undelivered). `local` mode keeps all three 9.1 gates unchanged.
- **R-9 (finding 9, decision 24).** Adopting a user's commit through `set_stage_head` sets 9.1's C-28 `floor` when that commit is unrecorded on the stage's line. That is intended: nothing below the user's commit is blamed. M9.2.8 tests it.
- **R-10 (finding 10; decisions 9 and 10, `open_pr`'s idempotence).** `open_pr`'s lookup filters `gh pr list --head <branch>` by `headRepositoryOwner.login == <repo owner>` and by the head repository being the base repository (`isCrossRepository == false`); a fork's PR never counts. The argv's `--json` list gains `headRepositoryOwner,isCrossRepository`.
- **R-11 (finding 11, decisions 13 and 17).** `[remote rejected]` is a host refusal: `the remote refused the push of <branch>: <reason>`. It holds the stage with an attention line and a wake note. It is never reported as `remote stage branch was rewritten by someone else`, which stays only for `[rejected] (non-fast-forward)`.
- **R-12 (R-1's open point, discard refs).** R-1's local discard also removes the run's `refs/anthrex/<run>/remote/*` (local private refs). It still never touches a remote.
- **R-13 (9.0.5's UI, for M9.2.15; decisions 40–42).**
  - In `pr` mode the priority-4 "ready to accept" alert never shows.
  - `AlertKey::Delivery` at priority 3, for: CI handed to the user, a PR closed without merging, `gh` logged out, a host op held after repeated failures, and review rounds over the cap. Unaddressed threads are not an alert, only a STATUS line.
  - The task panel's STATUS and RESULT show the task's PR (`#<n>` and its state) and its `fixes` text.
  - The plan review shows `delivered as <n> pull requests to <owner>/<name>` for a `pr` run.
  - The goal dialog (`tui/src/run_goal.rs:235`) gets the `delivery` field as a minimum arm in M9.2.2 (`None`); its UI is M9.2.15's.
- **File budgets (approved as suggested).** A move commit first puts `OpResult` in its own file (before M9.2.6 adds `OpResult::Host`); the `from: 0` and base-sync bodies go in `engine/delivery/sync.rs`, so `propagate.rs` and `merge.rs` gain one line each. Each split is its own move commit, with the `#[test]` counts unchanged and `git show --numstat` netting to about 0.

### Task M9.2.2

- **Protocol 13** (R-1's notes, correction 1; **renumbered to 14** by the merge with 9.0.6, see that heading): `PROTO_VERSION = 13` with its derivation in `proto/src/lib.rs`; `proto_version_is_twelve` became `proto_version_is_thirteen`. `HISTORY_VERSION = 4`. `docs/ROADMAP.md`'s protocol line gains "13 once milestone 9.2 merges" (not in the task's file list; the line is the one the brief's header derives from, and 9.1's protocol task raised it the same way).
- **What exists now.** `proto/src/delivery.rs` (new): `DeliveryMode`, `DeliveryProfile` (`remote` defaults to `origin`, `deny_unknown_fields`), `PrState`, `CiState`, `CiCategory`, `CheckRunInfo`, `ThreadCounts`, `StagePrInfo`, `DeliveryInfo`, and the history line's `StageOutcome` and `MergeMethod` (Interfaces names them in `StageLine` without a module; they are in `delivery.rs` because the daemon's model uses them too), all re-exported at the crate root. `RunRequest::{Start, StartGoal}.delivery`, `RunRequest::{Deliver, Watch}` appended **after `TaskDetail`**, `request::{DELIVER, WATCH}`; `PlanEdit::ReplyComment` last; `PlanTask.addresses`; `HoldKind::Fix` last; `RunInfo.delivery` last; `StageInfo.pr` last (in `proto/src/tiers.rs`, where 9.1 put `StageInfo`); `RepoProfile.delivery` (`skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none"`); `HistoryLine::Stage(StageLine)` last, `StageLine` in `history.rs`.
- **Minimum arms** (behaviour comes with the tasks named):
  - `driver/requests.rs`: `Start`/`StartGoal` bind `delivery: _` (M9.2.12's preflight reads it); `Deliver` and `Watch` are refused with `run deliver is not available yet` / `run watch is not available yet` under their labels (M9.2.7, M9.2.12).
  - `edits.rs`: `ReplyComment` is refused in a batch with `reply_comment is not available yet` (rule `30`; M9.2.10).
  - `edit_log.rs::describe_one`: `reply to <pr>:<thread>`, as the task says.
  - `engine/history.rs`, `history_io.rs::record_id`: the `Stage` line's `record_id`; `stats.rs`: a `stage` line is skipped.
  - Every struct literal the new fields broke got `delivery: None`, `pr: None` or `addresses: Vec::new()` (the snapshot, `scout/report.rs`'s proposal profile, `add_fix`, the integration review, triage, the CLI's `run start` and `run start --goal`, and the TUI's goal dialog, `tui/src/run_goal.rs:240`, as R-13 asks).
- **Deviations.**
  1. `PlanEdit` carries `#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]`: `addresses` took `AddTask { task: PlanTask }` past clippy 1.98's threshold. Boxing the task would change 48 constructors across every client and test; the crate already allows the lint where a value is built once per request (`ProfileReply`). The reason is in the enum's doc comment.

No assertion changed. 9.1's protocol task (`b325cdc`) edited the same files for the same reason.

#### Review of `628a932` (changes required, test pinning), and fixes

The `stage` literal is decision 44's keys plus Interfaces' `record_id`, under `"type": "stage"`. The fixtures now give every same-typed field a distinct value: `opened_at` 1700000100, `human_review_secs` 600, `merged_at` 1700009200, threads 2/3/4/5; `ci_rounds` 5, `review_rounds` 2, `sync_tasks` 3. **Mutants, each by swapping the two keys' serialized names, each red** (115 passed, 1 failed: the new test): `ThreadCounts` `new`↔`tasked`; `StagePrInfo` `opened_at`↔`human_review_secs`, `url`↔`base`, `merged_at`↔`opened_at`; `DeliveryInfo` `watching`↔`delivering`, `remote`↔`repo`; `StageLine` `ci_rounds`↔`review_rounds`, `time_to_open_secs`↔`human_review_secs`.

`anthrex-proto`: 116 tests, all pass; fmt and clippy 1.98 (host and Linux) clean.

#### A regression from `628a932`, fixed in `c79a418`

Running the whole `anthrex-daemon` library for M9.2.3 found two digest tests red since `628a932` (M9.2.2 ran only a filtered subset): `StageInfo.pr` was written as `"pr": null` into every stage of `run_status`'s digest, so `digest_shape_matches_the_interface` and `digest_lists_stages_with_heads_and_tier3_state` failed. `StageInfo.pr` and `RunInfo.delivery` are now `skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none"`, and `PlanTask.addresses` `skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty"`, so a local run's snapshot, plan and digest are written exactly as 9.1's. Pinned in `delivery_tests.rs`: the 9.1 `RunInfo` fixture re-serializes to the identical JSON, and an empty `addresses` writes no key; removing each of the three attributes makes a test red (115 passed, 1 failed, each time). The wire is unaffected: every field keeps `#[serde(default)]`, and MessagePack is written by name.

### Task M9.2.3

- **What exists now.**

Problems for the other keys: `must be between <a> and <b>` (and for a wrong type), `must be on_conflict or always`, `must be an array of GitHub logins` (decision 22's login pattern, `[bot]` allowed), `expected a boolean`, and `must be two integers between 50 and 5000, the first below the second`.

Refusals: `unknown key delivery.<k>; one of delivery.mode, delivery.remote`, `delivery.mode: must be pr or local`, `delivery.remote: <name> is not a valid remote name` (`git check-ref-format --branch`'s rule, applied in pure code: no git runs; the name shown redacted, see the review fixes below).

The mode stays `local` until M9.2.12 resolves it.

### Review of 9f86637..197a789 and fixes

The review accepted the six deviations, the merge, and c79a418's byte identity, and asked for six fixes, made in one commit:

1. **The production freeze path is tested.** `RunContext::from_config(data_dir, manager, &config::Config, roots)` (`run/driver/context.rs`) now builds the daemon's context from the loaded config (`[orchestrator]`, `[testing]`, `[delivery]`), and `lifecycle.rs` calls it instead of chaining `with_testing`/`with_delivery` itself, so the test covers lifecycle's wiring. New test `run/driver/adapt_goal_delivery_tests.rs::the_daemons_delivery_table_is_frozen_into_a_started_run`: a config whose `[delivery]` differs in every key (`ci_fix_max = 0`, `sync = always`, …) goes through `from_config`, `RunService::build_plan` builds a run (fast shape, a real git repository, agent binaries `/usr/bin/false`, never launched), and `run.delivery.limits` equals the table. Red first: it did not compile without `from_config`. Mutants, each red (1 failed): r1 `build.rs` passing `&config::Delivery::default()`; r7 `with_delivery` dropping its argument; r2 `from_config` without `.with_delivery(…)`.
2. **`SyncPolicy`'s spelling in `run.json` is pinned**: `run/delivery/mod.rs::tests::sync_policy_is_persisted_in_snake_case` checks `"on_conflict"` and `"always"` by literal JSON, both ways. Mutant r5 (the `rename_all` removed) is red. Pinning: it passed at once.
3. **The config hint is checked as displayed** (`Problem.to_string()`): see deviation 6. Pinning: it passed at once.
4. **Remote names match `git check-ref-format --branch`** (checked against git 2.50.1): a trailing `.` (`a.`, `x/a.`) and `HEAD` are now refused; `@` alone is now accepted, as git accepts it; `a/HEAD`, `HEAD/x`, `a.b` and `a@b` are accepted. Note that `git remote add` itself accepts more (`a.`, `HEAD`, `-x`); the brief's rule is `--branch`'s, which is kept.
5. **A refused remote is not echoed verbatim**: `redacted` replaces a URL's or an scp-style address's userinfo with `***@` and escapes control characters (`escape_debug`). New test `profile/tests_delivery.rs::a_refused_remote_is_redacted_in_the_error`: `https://u:tok@github.com/o/r` is shown as `https://***@github.com/o/r`, `u:tok@github.com:o/r` as `***@github.com:o/r`, and `a<TAB>b<ESC>[2Jc` as `a\tb\u{1b}[2Jc`, in `apply_edit`'s refusal and in `validate`. Red first: the test and the new cases of `profile_edit_refuses_an_unknown_delivery_key` failed before the change.
6. Deviations 5 and 6 above record `proposal_delivery.rs` and the hint stored as a `Problem`.

Counts after the fixes: `anthrex-config` 76; `anthrex-daemon --lib` 2195 → 2198, all pass; `anthrex-daemon --test '*'` 72 binaries, 605 tests, all pass. Build, fmt and clippy 1.98 (host and `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`) clean.

### Merge with main after 9.0.6 (2026-10-02)

`origin/main` at `bdfe635` (milestone 9.0.6, PR #28) was merged into this branch with a merge commit. 9.0.6 had raised `PROTO_VERSION` 12 → 13 for its actions and settings messages, the same number this branch's M9.2.2 had taken, so **9.2 now takes 14**.

- **Protocol 14.** `PROTO_VERSION = 14` in `proto/src/lib.rs`; the doc comment keeps 9.0.6's 12 → 13 entry and records 9.2's bump as 13 → 14 (9.0.6 merged first, `bdfe635`). `proto_version_is_thirteen` became `proto_version_is_fourteen`. `HISTORY_VERSION` stays 4 (9.0.6 added no history line). `docs/ROADMAP.md`'s protocol line now reads 13 for 9.0.6 and 14 for 9.2; the 9.2 row keeps main's dependency (9.0.7) and is `in progress`. Every earlier note that gives 9.2's protocol as 13 is annotated in place.
- **Variant order.** `RunRequest`: main's `Settings(SettingsRequest)` (13) keeps its index right after `TaskDetail`; 9.2's `Deliver` and `Watch` follow it, last. The same order in `request::{SETTINGS, DELIVER, WATCH}` and in `driver/requests.rs`'s match. The pins were extended, not loosened: `delivery_tests.rs::appended_variants_keep_their_indices` and `orch_tests_detail.rs` now expect `[…, "TaskDetail", "Settings", "Deliver", "Watch"]`, and 9.0.6's `settings_tests.rs::settings_variants_are_appended_last` checks `Settings` at its index (fourth from last) instead of last. `RunReply` gained nothing from 9.2, so its `Settings` is still last.
- **Fields.** `RunInfo` and `StageInfo` carry main's `actions` (skipped when empty) and then 9.2's `delivery` / `pr` (skipped when `None`). MessagePack is written by name, so the order is not on the wire. The snapshot builders fill both (`snapshot.rs`: `actions` from `available`, `delivery: None`; `snapshot_stages.rs`: `actions: Vec::new()` then filled, `pr: None`).
- **Other textual conflicts.** `config/src/lib.rs`: both `delivery` and main's `theme` (field, default, `read_*`, unknown-key arm). `daemon/src/lifecycle.rs`: `RunContext::from_config(…, &loaded_config, …)` (M9.2.3's review fix) followed by main's `.with_settings(LiveSettings::new(…), config_path)`; `from_config` already applies `[testing]` and `[delivery]`, so main's separate `.with_testing(…)` is not repeated. `profile/mod.rs`: both `tests_delivery` and main's `tests_live_runs`.
- **Semantic conflicts found by the build** (no textual conflict):
  1. `tui/src/app_tests/goal_form.rs` (9.0.6's goal-form tests) builds `RunRequest::StartGoal` literals: each gained `delivery: None`.
  2. `tui/src/profile_view_tests.rs` (9.0.6's Profile screen) mirrors the daemon's `EDIT_KEYS`, which 9.2 extended with `delivery.mode` and `delivery.remote`, and checks that the screen groups every key and every table key of a full profile. The mirror now lists both keys and `full_profile()` sets `delivery`; the three names (`delivery.mode`, `delivery.remote`, `delivery`) are excluded by a named constant, `UNGROUPED_UNTIL_M9_2_15`, so the gap stays visible. **For M9.2.15:** the Profile screen must group the `[delivery]` keys (and delete that constant). The screen's `value_literal` sends a `Choice`/`Text` value as a quoted TOML string (`"pr"`), but `proposal_delivery::edit` reads the bare text (`pr`, as `profile edit delivery.mode=pr` sends it), so the delivery rows need their own literal.
- **Checked, nothing to change now.** No exhaustive match either side added misses a variant of the other (the build is clean). The TUI's `tagged_not_sent` falls back to the usual toast for `Deliver`/`Watch`, which it does not send yet. 9.0.6's settings writer owns only `[orchestrator]` and keeps every other table, `[delivery]` included. **For M9.2.7 and M9.2.15:** 9.0.6's daemon-computed action menu (`engine/actions/`) offers `Accept` and `Discard` on a complete run whatever its delivery mode. In `pr` mode `Accept` must not be offered and `Discard` only on a complete run (decisions 38–39, ruling R-1); there are no `Deliver`/`Watch` actions yet. Harmless until M9.2.12 sets a run's mode to `pr`.
- **Wire coverage of both sides.** A follow-up commit adds `proto/src/delivery_actions_tests.rs`: a 9.0.6 (protocol 13) `RunInfo` decodes with no delivery and writes no `delivery` or `pr` key, a 9.1 `RunInfo` decodes with no actions, and a `RunInfo` that carries both sides' fields (run, task and stage `actions`, `delivery`, a stage's `pr`) round-trips through MessagePack and JSON with each key pinned by literal JSON.
- **Verification.** `cargo build --workspace --all-targets` is clean. Tests: `anthrex-proto` 127 at the merge commit and 130 with the follow-up, `anthrex-config` 121, `anthrex-tui --lib` 1136, the `anthrex` binary's unit tests 57, `anthrex-daemon --lib` 2268, all pass. The daemon's `server_runs`, `lifecycle`, `lifecycle_resume`, `profile_store` and `server` binaries pass. The CLI's `profile_cli_tui`, `run_cli`, `run_e2e_settings_live`, `scout_service`, `scout_service_planner` and `run_e2e_orch tui_flow` pass. `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo +1.99 clippy --workspace --all-targets -D warnings` (host and `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`) and `cargo +1.98 check --workspace --all-targets` are clean.

### Task M9.2.4

- **What exists now.** `crates/daemon/src/host/` (`pub mod host;` in `daemon/src/lib.rs`): `mod.rs` (the `CodeHost` trait, its request, outcome and view types, `HostError`, the timeouts, `VIEW_TEXT_MAX` and `VIEW_ITEMS_MAX = 200`), `runner.rs` (`Program`, `Capture`, `RunOutput`, the `Runner` trait, `SystemRunner`: the only `Command::new` in `host/`), `gh.rs` (`GhHost`, `THREADS_QUERY`, the `--json` field lists, the `gh` methods), `gh_preflight.rs` (decision 17's six checks with the exact refusal texts), `gh_git.rs` (`push`, `fetch` with decision 24's adoption, the branch delete), `gh_parse.rs` (pure: `classify`, the view, list, comment, permission, version and `git push --porcelain` readers), `allow.rs` (pure: decision 7's allow-list), `remote.rs` (pure: remote URLs, `redact`), and test-only `scripted.rs` (`ScriptedRunner`). Tests: `tests.rs`, `tests_parse.rs`, `tests_allow.rs`, `tests_git.rs`.
- **Deviations** (each forced by evidence or by a ruling; nothing in a design decision was changed silently):
  1. **Files.** `gh.rs` came to 782 lines after `cargo fmt`, so preflight went to `gh_preflight.rs` and the `git` side to `gh_git.rs` (AGENTS.md rule 8); the tests are four files, not two (`tests_parse.rs` and `tests_git.rs` added). The Verification grep for force (`grep -rnE '"--force|"\+' …/gh.rs …/remote.rs`) should also name `gh_git.rs` and `gh_preflight.rs`; run over `host/gh*.rs`, `remote.rs` and `runner.rs` it prints nothing (the fetch refspec's `+` is a `char` constant, `FETCH_UPDATE`).
  2. **Shared code: stdout kept on a failed git.** `worktree::run_git` drops stdout when git exits non-zero, and only `git push --porcelain`'s stdout (`!\t<src>:<dst>\t[rejected] (non-fast-forward)` against `[remote rejected] (<reason>)`, observed with git 2.50.1) tells decision 13's rewrite from ruling R-11's refusal; stderr carries only `hint:` lines, which `advice.pushUpdateRejected = false` removes. Added `subprocess::run_captured_keeping` (new `subprocess/keep.rs`, 41 lines, so `subprocess.rs` stays at 575) and `worktree::run_git_keeping_stdout` (one new private `Capture::Keeping` arm in `run_git_capturing`; every other caller is unchanged). Red first: with `run_git_keeping_stdout` delegating to `run_git_with_cap`, `push_to_a_rewritten_remote_is_rejected_never_forced` failed with `Err(Failed("hint: See the 'Note about fast-forwards' …"))` where `Ok(Rejected { reason: "non-fast-forward" })` was expected.
  3. **`git fetch` gains `--no-write-fetch-head --refmap=`.** Observed (git 2.50.1): the table's `git fetch --no-tags <remote> +refs/heads/<b>:refs/anthrex/<run>/remote/<name>` from a *named* remote also updates `refs/remotes/<remote>/<b>` (git's opportunistic remote-tracking update) and writes `.git/FETCH_HEAD`, against decision 13's "never `refs/remotes/*`". `--refmap=` (empty) stops the first, `--no-write-fetch-head` (git 2.29; runs need 2.38) the second; `fetch_adopts_only_a_descendant` pins both (red without `--refmap=`). **For the controller:** `git push <remote> …` also updates `refs/remotes/<remote>/anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>` (git's own behaviour, observed; no flag stops it short of pushing to a URL). Nothing reads it, but R-1/R-12's local discard may want to remove `refs/remotes/<remote>/anthrex/<run>/*` as well (local refs only, no host call).
  4. **`THREADS_QUERY` is R-2's extended query** (exact text in `host/gh.rs`): `reviewThreads(first: 100) { nodes { isResolved path line comments(first: 50) { nodes { fullDatabaseId body diffHunk author { __typename login } } } } }`, then `reviews(last: 100)` and `comments(last: 100)` (newest, for the watermark) with `fullDatabaseId`, `state`/`body` and `author { __typename login }`. A thread's comments stay `first: 50` so its first comment (the thread key) is always read. `owner` and `name` are passed with `-f` (raw), not the table's `-F` (typed: a numeric owner such as `1234` would be sent as an `Int`, and `-F` reads a value starting with `@` as a file); `number` stays `-F`.
  5. **`gh pr view --json`** asks only `state,mergedAt,mergeCommit,baseRefName,headRefOid,mergeable,reviewDecision,statusCheckRollup` (R-2: reviews and comments come from the GraphQL read, M9.2.1 finding 1). **`gh pr list --json`** adds `headRepositoryOwner,isCrossRepository` (R-10); a row of another owner or of a fork is not ours. Of ours, an open PR is preferred, else the highest number (returned with its state).
  6. **`PushOutcome::Refused { reason }`** is appended (R-11): `reason` is the whole text `the remote refused the push of anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>: <git's reason>`. `Rejected { reason }` stays only for `[rejected]` (`non-fast-forward`, `fetch first`).
  7. **`MergeCommit` is `{ oid }`**: `gh pr view` gives no parent count (M9.2.1 finding 3; R-4 counts them locally after the base fetch, M9.2.11).
  8. **`AllowCtx { run_id: Option<&str>, remote, base_branch: Option<&str>, repo: Option<&str> }`**: `repo` (`<owner>/<name>`) is added so every `gh` repository command must name this repository; `run_id` is `None` for preflight and the run-less `gh` reads (a run-scoped git command is then refused); `base_branch` is only preflight's `ls-remote`. `GhHost` takes the run from the request (`PushReq`, `DeleteBranchReq`), from `OpenPrReq.head` (`anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>`), or from `FetchReq.into` (`refs/anthrex/<run>/remote/<name>`).
  9. **`classify(stderr)`** takes no exit code (`run_git` reports none). `Could not resolve to a …` (GraphQL) is `NotFound`; `Could not resolve host` (a network failure) is `Failed`, not `NotFound` as decision 11's bare `Could not resolve` would read. A 403 that is not a rate limit is `Failed` (finding 5). R-6's `You are not logged into any accounts` is `Auth`.
  10. **`gh --version` is pinned at ≥ 2.92.0** (Risks, "Pin the minimum `gh` version"), as part of preflight 2, with two texts not in "Messages": `gh <v> is older than 2.92.0, the oldest version anthrex is checked against; upgrade it, or use --delivery local` and `gh --version printed no version (<line>); install gh 2.92.0 or later, or use --delivery local`. A `gh --version` that exits non-zero gets preflight 2's "not installed" text.
  11. **The runner.** `Program` carries no path: `SystemRunner::new(gh, git)` holds the binaries; `GhHost::new(runner, gh, git)` keeps `gh` for preflight 2's text and `git` for the adoption's 9.1 `git::refs_tx::cas`, which runs through `run::git` (so `run_git`), not through the `Runner` (it needs stdin). So `FakeGh` and `ScriptedRunner` never see the swap; the targets it may move are checked by `allow::local_run_ref` (this run's `stage-<n>` or `integration` only).
  12. **Request semantics the brief left open.** `Adopt.local_ref` is a branch name (`anthrex/<run>/stage-1`); `LocalMoved.local` is the moved ref's current commit (read with an allow-listed `rev-parse`; empty when gone). `ReplyReq.body` excludes the marker; `GhHost` posts `<body>\n\n<marker>`. A conversation reply's `--body-file` is a 0600 file in `std::env::temp_dir()`, removed after the call. `failed_logs` keeps `min(64 KiB, (max − 64) / 2)` bytes of head and the rest of `max − 64` as tail, with the line `… <n> bytes of the log cut here …` between, so the file never passes `max_bytes`; mode 0600. A GraphQL `author: null` (a deleted account) reads as `ghost`, not a bot.
  13. **Unknown values.** A check run's unknown `status` or `conclusion`, and a status context's unknown `state`, are pending (R-5); an unknown `__typename`, PR `state`, `mergeable`, review `state`, or any missing field is `Rejected("gh output changed: <field>")`.
  14. **Two fixtures are constructed, not recorded** (no `gh` was run in this task; for the manual check to confirm): `host/fixtures/pr_list_head_owner.json` is `pr_list_head.json` with R-10's two fields added (`headRepositoryOwner: {id, login}`, `isCrossRepository`; the second row made an `OPEN` fork PR); `host/fixtures/threads_query_response_extended.json` is `threads_query_response_full_database_id.json` plus `reviews` and `comments` built from `pr_view_conflicting_changes_requested.json` (bodies, states, logins; `__typename` `Bot` for `github-actions` and `copilot-pull-request-reviewer`), with the conversation comments' ids from `rest_issue_comments.json` and invented review ids. The recorded `pr_list_head.json` (no owner fields) and `threads_query_response.json` (deprecated `databaseId` only) are used to show both are refused as `gh output changed`.
  15. The allow-list also refuses git's `--no-verify` outright (not in decision 7's list; every shape check refuses it anyway).

### Task M9.2.5

- **What exists now.** `crates/daemon/src/host/fake/` (`pub mod fake;` in `host/mod.rs`, compiled into the binary, not test-only): `mod.rs` (`FakeHost`, `GhHost<FakeGh>` by delegation; `FakeGh`, the `Runner`: `git` to `SystemRunner` unchanged, `gh` interpreted; the landing guard; the login and rate-limit gates), `gh_pr.rs` (`gh --version`, `auth status`, `repo view`, `pr list|create|view|comment|edit`, `run view|rerun`), `gh_api.rs` (`THREADS_QUERY`, the two `--paginate` listings, the thread reply `POST`, the permission read), `github.rs` (`FakeGithub`, its `github.json` under a blocking `flock` on `github.lock`, `calls.jsonl`, `forbidden.jsonl`, and `Bare`, every git command on the bare repository through `worktree::run_git`), `rules.rs` (`CiRule`, `FileContains`, `CiRun`: scripted CI), `ctl.rs` (`FakeGithubCtl`, `MergeMethodArg`). Tests: `tests.rs` (the rig), `tests_ci_merge.rs`, `tests_lifecycle.rs`.
- **Deviations** (none touches a design decision):
  1. **Files.** `gh_pr.rs` and `gh_api.rs` are the split "File sizes" names for the `gh` interpreter (`mod.rs` alone would pass 450); the tests are three files, not one (AGENTS.md rule 8).
  2. **`FakeGh` is in process**, as decision 14 and Interfaces (`FakeHost { inner: GhHost<FakeGh> }`) say: no fake `gh` binary exists and nothing named `gh` is ever spawned (`FakeGh`'s `SystemRunner` holds `/nonexistent/anthrex-fake-host/gh` and runs only `git`). `ANTHREX_GH_BIN` plays no part in a `FakeHost` (M9.2.12's `select.rs` builds `FakeHost::new(dir)` for `ANTHREX_CODE_HOST=fake`).
  3. **The two logs** hold the argv as the `Runner` sees it, without the program (`["pr","view","1",…]`). Every `gh` call goes to `calls.jsonl` first, an unsupported or a landing one too; `git` calls are not logged (decision 14: "every `gh` call").
  4. **`CiRule` gains `pending: u32`** (`#[serde(default)]`): the run reads `IN_PROGRESS` (conclusion `""`) for that many `gh pr view` calls, again after each rerun. M9.2.4's notes require a rerun of a running run to answer `already running`, which needs a running run. Builders: `CiRule::new(check, conclusion)`, `.when(path, text)`, `.log(…)`, `.failing(&[…])`, `.times(n)`, `.pending(n)`. A rule concluding `Error` is shown as a `StatusContext` (`state: ERROR`), the only shape with that value.
  5. **The panic list is a superset of decision 14's:** `pr merge` (any flags; with `--auto`: "enable auto-merge"), `pr review` (any form: "approve"), and a `gh api` call whose path has a `merge` or `merges` segment ("merge"), a `reviews` segment ("approve"), method `PUT` (any path: "merge", in `-X PUT`, `-XPUT` and `--method=PUT` forms), or a `graphql` query that contains `mutation` (read from `-f`/`-F` values, `-F query=@<file>` and `--input <file>`; `…AutoMerge…` is "enable auto-merge", `…review…` "approve", any other "merge"). Only positional path arguments are checked, so a reply body that mentions `/merge` is not a landing (tested). The argv goes to `forbidden.jsonl` (under the lock) before the panic.
  6. **Scripted CI semantics.** Checks are the distinct `check` names of the rules, in order. A check's first sight on a head makes one Actions run (new run and job ids); the first rule of that check whose `fail_if` holds on the head (`git cat-file blob <head>:<path>` contains the text; no `fail_if` always holds) and whose `times` are not used up turns it red with its conclusion, log and failing tests, otherwise it is `SUCCESS`. `gh run rerun <id> --failed` re-decides a red run under the same id. `--log-failed` prints gh's observed line shape (`<check>\tUNKNOWN STEP\t<time>Z <text>`, a byte-order mark before the first timestamp), the rule's log lines and then `--- FAIL: <test> (0.01s)` per failing test; a green run prints nothing.
  7. **GitHub's reactions are applied before every answer** (`FakeGithub::refresh`): an open PR's head follows its branch; when a merged PR's head branch is gone, open PRs based on it are retargeted to its base (M9.2.1 check 5), whoever deleted it (`FakeGithubCtl::merge(…, true)`, or anthrex's own `delete_branch`); then an open PR whose head or base branch is gone is closed (check 5's second sentence). Both are **from the documentation**.
  8. **Mergeability**: an open PR is `MERGEABLE` when `git merge-tree --write-tree <base tip> <head>` is clean, `CONFLICTING` when not; any PR not open reads `UNKNOWN` (observed for a merged PR in `pr_view_merged.json`; for a closed one, assumed).
  9. **Bots.** A `FakeGithubCtl` login ending in `[bot]` is a bot: REST shows it with `[bot]` and `type: Bot`, GraphQL without the suffix and `__typename: Bot` (observed, M9.2.1 check 2). `gh` posts as `FAKE_LOGIN` (`tester`, one of M9.2.1's fixture logins).
  10. **Ids.** PR numbers from 1; comment and review ids from 5 000 000 001 (past `u32::MAX`, as `rest_issue_comments.json`'s 5 093 042 634 already is); Actions run and job ids from 28 000 000 001 (the fixtures' range).
  11. **The user's commits and merges** never use an index (rule 11 scrubs `GIT_INDEX_FILE`): `commit` is `hash-object -w --stdin`, a recursive `mktree -z`, `commit-tree` and a compare-and-swap `update-ref`; `merge` is `merge-tree --write-tree` then `commit-tree` with two parents (merge commit, message `Merge pull request #<n> from <owner>/<head>`), one (squash, `<title> (#<n>)`), or one per replayed commit (rebase, `merge-tree --merge-base=<c>^`). Author `fake-user`. A merge that conflicts panics (test setup).
  12. **Texts that are documented or constructed, not observed** (for the manual check): both rate-limit texts (documented; REST commands — `run`, the listings, the reply, the permission — get `gh: API rate limit exceeded for user ID 1. (HTTP 403)`, the others `GraphQL: API rate limit exceeded for user ID 1.`; `gh --version` asks GitHub nothing and does not use up a limited call); the logged-out text of a non-`auth` command (`To get started with GitHub CLI, please run:  gh auth login`, gh's message as remembered); `gh pr create`'s refusals (missing branch, no commits, an open PR for the same head and base) and its stderr line; `gh pr edit`'s refusals (a base that does not exist, a PR that is not open); `run <id> cannot be rerun; it has no failed jobs`; `run <id> is still in progress; logs will be available when it is complete`; the REST 404 (`gh: Not Found (HTTP 404)` with GitHub's JSON on stdout); the reply `POST`'s created-comment JSON (documented, no fixture); `--paginate` printing one array per page of 30, back to back (`[…][…]`, gh without `--slurp`); `gh auth status`'s stdout. `failed to get run: HTTP 404: Not Found (actions/runs/<id>)` is the observed text with the URL cut to its path, so no API host literal enters the source (Verification's last grep).
  13. **Extra API**: `FakeHost::dir()`, `FakeGithubCtl::dir()`; `FakePr` carries `comments`, `reviews`, `threads` and `replies` (every comment anthrex posted, also kept in the conversation or its thread); `prs()` returns them all. There is no `log_out` (decision 11's "auth lost while watching" test, M9.2.8, may add one).
  14. **Left to their tasks**: `select.rs` and `ANTHREX_CODE_HOST` (M9.2.12), the hidden `anthrex run fake-github` (M9.2.14), each harness's assert at drop that `forbidden.jsonl` does not exist (M9.2.16, M9.2.17).
- **Tests** (11, all through real git and a local bare repository; the rig asserts that `remote get-url` and `get-url --push` both resolve to the bare path before anything pushes, with `insteadOf` and `pushInsteadOf` set, so no test can reach GitHub): `tests.rs` `fake_host_panics_on_merge_approve_and_auto_merge` (the brief's five under `catch_unwind`, each with decision 14's exact text, `forbidden.jsonl` five lines; nine more shapes in a second directory; a reply mentioning `/merge` does not panic), `fake_gh_refuses_unknown_commands` (nine argv, `FakeGh: unsupported: gh …`, no `forbidden.jsonl`), `fake_github_state_survives_a_reopen` (PR, comments, the reply, a permission and the id counter through a second `open`; a bot's GraphQL login), `fake_rate_limit_fails_the_next_calls_with_githubs_text`, `fake_github_changes_are_serialized_by_the_lock` (two threads, 50 comments, none lost); `tests_lifecycle.rs` `fake_pr_lifecycle_through_gh_host` (preflight → push (and `UpToDate`) → `open_pr` (and `existed`) → `view_pr` (head, a green check with its Actions run, `MERGEABLE`, a thread) → `reply` (and the marker found the second time) → `retarget`; deleting an open PR's head closes it), `fake_mergeable_is_conflicting_when_the_base_conflicts` (and `force_rewrite`).

#### Task M9.2.4, fix round 1 (on `d1efefc`)

The controller's rulings, each with its test; every change is shown red by a mutant (restored from a copy after each run).
- **I1.** All three push forms (`push`, the preflight dry run, the branch delete) pass `--no-follow-tags --recurse-submodules=no` after `--porcelain`, so a user's `push.followTags` sends no tag and `push.recurseSubmodules` no submodule commit; the fetch passes `--no-recurse-submodules --no-auto-maintenance` after `--no-tags`. The allow-list's shapes require them (a push or fetch without them is refused). `push_to_a_rewritten_remote_is_rejected_never_forced` sets `push.followTags = true` and `push.recurseSubmodules = on-demand` and tags both pushed commits with annotated tags: the bare remote ends with no tag (red with the flag swapped for `-v`).
- **I2.** `gh_parse::push_outcome`: only `[rejected]` is `PushOutcome::Rejected` and `[remote rejected]` `Refused`; any other `!` summary (`[remote failure] (remote failed to report status)`, `[no match]`, …) is `Err(HostError::Failed("git push of <branch> failed: <summary>"))`, retried when due. New pure test `tests_limits.rs::only_rejected_and_remote_rejected_are_push_outcomes` with constructed porcelain lines.
- **I3.** `tests_git.rs`'s `Rig::fetch` deletes every `refs/remotes/*` before each fetch and asserts none exists after it (and no `FETCH_HEAD`), for every fetch of `fetch_adopts_only_a_descendant`, the base fetches included. Red with `--refmap=` removed (`the fetch wrote a remote-tracking ref`).
- **m1.** The allow-list refuses a remote starting with `-` (push, fetch, `ls-remote`, `config --get`), even when the context names it; test `allow_list_refuses_a_remote_named_like_an_option_and_the_old_push_shapes`.
- **m2.** `OpenPrReq` and `FetchReq` gain `run_id: String`; the allow check takes the run from it, not from `head` or `into`. `allow_list_refuses_merge_approve_and_auto_merge` adds a PR head and a fetch ref of another run (`r9999`): both `Forbidden`, the runner untouched.
- **m3.** `gh` also gets `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0` (AGENTS.md rule 11: a git that gh runs takes no optional lock); `every_method_builds_its_exact_argv` checks the environment.
- **m4.** `THREADS_QUERY` reads `reviewThreads(last: 100)` (the newest threads); each thread's `comments(first: 50)` is unchanged, so its first comment (the key) is read. `every_method_builds_its_exact_argv` now pins the query byte for byte as a literal; `FakeGh` answers the last 100 threads.
- **m5.** `remote::redact` is M9.2.3's rule: a scheme URL's userinfo, and an scp-form's when it holds a `:` (`u:tok@github.com:o/r` → `***@github.com:o/r`), become `***`; only control characters are escaped. Test `tests_limits.rs::redaction_hides_a_token_in_every_url_form`.
- **m6.** `tests_limits.rs::a_view_keeps_the_newest_items_of_each_class`: 250 reviews, comments and threads; each class keeps the newest 200, newest first.

#### Task M9.2.5, fix round 1 (on `bfa3a5f`)

- **I1: the landing guard knows every spelling.** It moved to `host/fake/landing.rs` (173 lines; `fake/mod.rs` is 346). `gh pr` skips `-R`/`--repo` in all four forms (`-R o/r`, `-Ro/r`, `--repo o/r`, `--repo=o/r`) before the subcommand. `gh api` flags are read as pflag reads them: long `--flag v` and `--flag=v`; shorthands `-Xv`, `-X=v`, `-X v`, and combined (`-iXPUT`, where `-i` is `--include`). The value flags are `-X -f -F -H -q -t -p` and `--method --raw-field --field --header --input --jq --template --preview --cache --hostname`, so no flag value is taken for a path. Every positional is a path once `scheme://host/`, a leading `/` and a query are removed. A `merge`, `merges` or `reviews` segment in any of them is a landing, and so is `PUT` in any case. A body containing `mutation` is a landing when any path is `graphql` or `api/graphql` (GitHub Enterprise's endpoint). `-F k=@f` and `--input f` bodies are read relative to the `dir` the `Runner` gets, never the process's cwd. Run against the new table, the old guard missed 15 spellings: `-X=PUT`, `-iXPUT`, the 5 file-read and full-URL GraphQL forms, 3 glued `-f`/`-F`/`--raw-field=` bodies, and 5 `pr -R`/`--repo` forms. It also panicked on 3 harmless calls (`--template merge`, `-p merge`, `--cache merge -q merges`).
- **The allow-list refuses every one of those argv** (`assert_refused`: `HostError::Forbidden` for each landing case, with a context naming this run and repository). No `allow.rs` change was needed.
- **m1.** The guard runs before `calls.jsonl` is written. A landing ask writes both logs and then panics; if either write fails, the error is named in the panic text, never returned. A test with `calls.jsonl` made a directory shows it.
- **m2.** `read_lines` (`FakeGithubCtl::calls`, `forbidden`) takes the lock.
- **m3.** The five-case test also asserts that `calls.jsonl` holds the five landing argv.
- **m4.** The shape test also checks a merged PR's view against `pr_view_merged.json` (`state`, `mergeable` equal; `mergedAt`, `mergeCommit {oid}` shaped alike), and an `Error` rule's `StatusContext` against `status_check_rollup_status_context.json`.
- **m5.** `fake_close_reopen_resolve_and_the_edit_refusals`: resolve; close; the closed-PR edit refusal (classified `Failed`); an edit to the base it already has succeeds even when the PR is closed; reopen; the missing-base refusal (`NotFound`); a PR whose head branch was deleted is closed and cannot be reopened.
- **Files.** The panic tests moved from `tests.rs` to `tests_landing.rs` (321 lines); `tests.rs` is 354, `tests_ci_merge.rs` 309, `tests_lifecycle.rs` 458, `github.rs` 398.
- **Red, then mutants.** The new spelling test failed against the old guard at `-X=PUT`; a one-off run of the old guard recorded the full list of misses above. 8 mutants of the new code each turned a test red: calls written before the guard (5 tests), no `-R` skip, no URL stripping, files read from the cwd, `--template` not a value flag, `-X=v` not stripped, combined shorthands not walked, no `api/graphql`.
- **Commands run:** `cargo test -p anthrex-daemon --lib -- host::` (31 pass: task 4's 17 and the fake's 14), `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo +1.99 clippy -p anthrex-daemon --all-targets -D warnings` (host and `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`), `cargo +1.98 check -p anthrex-daemon --all-targets`: clean. The Verification greps print nothing.

### Task M9.2.6

- **What exists now.** `run/delivery/` (pure): `mod.rs` (the model: `RunDelivery { mode, repo, limits, watching, poll_base_secs, stages, permissions, base_synced, base_sync_due, failures }` with `stage`, `pr` and decision 36's `delivering(stage_count)`; `StageDelivery`, `PrRecord`, `Watermark`, `CiSeen`, `CiPhase`, `CiRecord`, `ThreadRecord`, `ThreadState`, `Batch`; `BODY_MAX_CHARS`, `FAILURES_BEFORE_ATTENTION`), `ops.rs` (`HostOp`, `HostResult`), `body.rs` (`pr_title`, `pr_body`, `stacked_on`), `quote.rs` (`comment`, `ci_log`, `login`), `snapshot.rs` (`delivery_info`, `stage_pr_info`, 9.2's `fix_text`). `OpKind::Host { repo, op }` and `OpResult::Host(HostResult)` appended last; 9.1's `FixOf` gains `Ci`, `Review`, `Base`, and `BisectRecord` gains `#[serde(default)] ci: Option<(u16, String)>`; `report_escape::fenced` is `pub(crate)`. The snapshot fills `RunInfo.delivery` and each `StageInfo.pr` (`snapshot.rs`, one call each); `TaskInfo.fixes` carries decision 41's texts.
- **Move commit first** (ruling "File budgets"): `5747b3f` moved `OpResult` to `engine/op_result.rs` (`git show --numstat`: +182/−176 and +3/−1 in `engine/mod.rs`; the lib's `#[test]` count unchanged at 2299). `engine/ops.rs` is 429 after `OpKind::Host`.
- **Deviations** (none changes a design decision):
  1. **M9.2.1's facts supersede the brief's file list**: `StageInfo.pr` is filled in `snapshot.rs`'s stage loop (beside 9.0.6's `actions`), not in `snapshot_stages.rs::stage_infos`, so the orchestrator's digest, which shares `stage_infos`, stays 9.1's until M9.2.13 adds its own `delivery` block. `fix_text` is `engine/fixes.rs:137` as M9.2.1 recorded.
  2. **`engine::fix_text(fixes)` became `fix_text(run, fixes)`**: a review fix's text names its threads' authors (`thread by @alice`), which only `RunDelivery`'s thread records hold, and a base sync's names `run.base_branch`. Its three callers changed one line each (`fixes.rs::add_fix`, `snapshot.rs`, and `report_tiers.rs`, not in the file list). `snapshot.rs::task_info` has no run, so `run_info` sets `info.fixes` beside `info.actions`.
  3. **Minimum arms outside the file list, forced by the new variants**: `engine/bisect.rs` (`ci: None` in its one `BisectRecord` literal); `reconcile/mod.rs` (`OpKind::Host { .. }` joins the `NotStarted` row: decision 10's row, which M9.2.7 owned, is exactly this); `driver/ops.rs` (`OpKind::Host` answers `failed("host ops are not executed yet")` until M9.2.12's executor; nothing emits one before M9.2.7); `engine/tests/fixture.rs::op_name` (`"Host"`).
  4. **`PrRecord.checks: Vec<CheckSeen { name, state, ci_run }>`** (`#[serde(default)]`) is added to Interfaces' `PrRecord`: `StagePrInfo.ci` and `.checks` ("the most recent head's checks") have no other source in the model (the watermark keeps only the CI acted on). M9.2.8 fills it from each view. A red check's `fix_task` is the newest `CiRecord` of the PR's pushed head whose `ci_runs` hold the check's Actions run (any record of that head, for a check with no Actions run).
  5. **`CiSeen { failing: Vec<String>, at }`** (Interfaces gives only "the failing check-run names acted on, and when"); `ThreadState` and `CiPhase` are snake-case serde (`{"tasked": {"task": …}}`, `"to_user"`).
  6. **The PR body's escaping is stricter than `list_item_text`** (decision 21 names `report_escape::list_item_text` and `\|`). `list_item_text` normalises line starts only, and in a PR body every agent string sits mid-line, where `<!--` would hide the rest of the body, `@<login>` would notify that user, and `*`, `_`, `[`, `` ` `` and `<` would format or link. So `body.rs::md` puts the text on one line (`safe_text::one_line`), backslash-escapes `` \ ` * _ [ ] < > & ~ | # `` (CommonMark renders each as itself), and turns `@` into `＠` (U+FF20; GitHub's mention filter does not read it, and it is not a hidden character). Test names and protected paths are code spans one backtick longer than any run in them (`|` escaped inside a table cell, as GFM requires). `#<n>` autolinks are left (the brief does not ask); the stack's own `#<pr>` is the one intended link. The marker line is the only HTML comment in a body (tested).
  7. **Which tasks a body lists**: the stage's tasks that are not cancelled, in plan order (at PR open they are all merged or reported, decision 19); the title's `(+<k> more)` counts the same set.
  8. **Body texts the template leaves open**: `<k> rounds` and `<k> signals` are pluralised (`1 round`, `1 signal`); the review cell is `override` when the task merged without approval, else the last round's verdict (`approve`, `changes`, or `none` with `0 rounds`); the named test is the last proof's test, else the plan's `test_to_write`, else `–`; `atomic task` has no parentheses when the plan gave no reason; `, <k> shards` is shown only for more than one shard; `Tier 3 on this head:` is `none` with no tier-3 record on the head, and `not run (the profile has no check, so the run is unverified)` when the profile has no `check` (decision 19's "its body says so"); the stack names the **nearest lower open** stage (`based on stage <m>, #<pr>`), which is `n − 1` unless a stage between was skipped or merged.
  9. **"Tiers run" counts the records 9.1 keeps**: tiers 1 and 2 are every `TierRecord` on the stage's tasks' checks; tier 3 is 0 or 1, because 9.1 keeps only `StageFull.last` per stage (no tier-3 run counter exists). **For the controller:** a true count needs one counter where `full.rs:350` sets `last` (a 9.1 file this task does not own).
  10. **Protected files** come from the model, which has no changed-path list: a task changes a protected path only when its `owns` names it exactly (M8a decision 56), so the body lists the paths a task of the stage names exactly that `profile.protected` matches or that were protected at the base (`run.protected_files`). An exact name that the task did not in fact change is still listed.
  11. **The 60 000-character cut**: rows are dropped from the end with `| … and <k> more tasks | | | | | | |`; when no row is left and the body still does not fit (one title of 70 000 characters), the text before the footer is cut so the marker and the footer stay (tested).
  12. **`quote.rs` also drops hidden format characters and turns control characters other than `\n` and `\t` into spaces** (`safe_text`'s rule, keeping tabs for logs and hunks), so a quote cannot reorder or redraw what a reader sees. A comment cut at 8 000 characters gets the line `(cut to 8000 characters)` after its fence. **`ci_log` keeps the log's last 48 KiB characters (`CI_LOG_MAX_CHARS`), not 8 000**: decision 18 quotes the last 48 KiB of a log to the decider through `ci_log`, and a log fails at its end; a cut log gets `(cut to its last 49152 characters)`. The check names in its label are put on one line.
  13. **The PR title** (task 4's carried ruling) is `safe_text::one_line`'d after it is composed (no `\n`, `\r`, control or bidi character), then cut to 256 characters; the title test runs `allow::check` on the `gh pr create` argv with it (passes) and with a raw `\n` title (`Forbidden`).
  14. **Fixture**: `run/delivery/m9_1_run.json` was written by a throwaway test (then removed) that ran 9.1's bisect scenario (`engine/tests/bisect.rs::bisected` + `answer(4)`: a stage record with tier-3 and bisect records, a `bisect` fix task `fix1`) and serialized the `Run` **before** this task changed the model, with the one key 9.2 had added by then (`delivery`, M9.2.3) removed. That is the `run.json` 9.1 (`fe1a381`) and 9.0.6 (`bdfe635`) write for it: `git diff fe1a381 bdfe635` touches no `run/model*.rs`, `orch/` model or `proto/src/run.rs` file, and M9.2.2's `PlanTask.addresses` is skipped when empty. No local path, login or email is in it (paths are the fixtures' `/tmp/…`).
- **Tests** (15 new, all pure): `run/delivery/tests_body.rs` `pr_title_is_exact`, `pr_body_is_exact_for_a_fixed_run`, `pr_body_names_the_base_branch_when_no_lower_pr_is_open`, `pr_body_ranks_look_here_first`, `pr_body_says_none_when_nothing_needs_a_look`, `pr_body_is_cut_at_60000_characters_with_a_more_row`, `pr_body_escapes_agent_text`; `tests_snapshot.rs` `quote_fence_is_longer_than_any_backtick_run_in_the_comment`, `quote_labels_the_author_and_hides_a_bad_login`, `stage_pr_info_counts_threads_and_ci` (and `delivery_info`, `delivering`, and both through the snapshot), `fixes_texts_for_ci_review_and_base`; `tests.rs` `old_run_json_loads_with_local_delivery`, `the_delivery_model_round_trips_through_run_json`, `host_ops_and_results_round_trip_through_the_journal`; `engine/tests/fixes.rs` `a_ci_fix_task_logs_its_text` (`add_fix`'s history and log lines). M9.2.3's `sync_policy_is_persisted_in_snake_case` moved from `mod.rs`'s inline module to `tests.rs` (same path). **Red first:** against stubs returning empty text and `None`, 12 of the 18 tests in `run::delivery` and `engine::tests::fixes` failed (the three round-trip and loading tests and the two older ones passed, the model being in place). **Mutants**, each restored from a copy, each red: a fixed three-backtick fence; `|` not escaped; `@` kept; any login accepted; the CI record's head filter dropped; no row dropped; the title not one-lined; look-here ranked by task, not reason; a review text with the raw login; no `StageInfo.pr` in the snapshot; a comment not cleaned; `Run.delivery` without `#[serde(default)]`; `delivery_info` in local mode; 9.1's `bisect of` text changed. One survived as equivalent: `BisectRecord.ci` without `#[serde(default)]` (serde defaults a missing `Option` field to `None` anyway; the attribute stays, as Interfaces writes it).

The purity grep prints nothing for `run/delivery` (`run/engine/delivery` does not exist yet). No `gh` ran, no daemon was started, no process was spawned but cargo's.

The controller's rulings, each with its test; the deviations they change are named.

#### Task M9.2.6, fix round 2 (on `2889375`)

- **The settings regression from fix round 1.** `config/src/settings/validate.rs`'s `unsafe_char` used `is_hidden_format`, so once fix round 1 widened it, a stored note with `⚠️` (U+26A0 U+FE0F) or a soft hyphen refused every settings save with `its note holds a control character`. Rule now (controller): settings validation never refuses a hidden format character. New `config::settings::cleaned(doc)` drops them from each model's name and note and from the orchestrator default's model (it names one of them, so both stay equal); `save` cleans first, then validates, writes, and runs 9.0.6's read-back guard against the cleaned doc, so the guard and the file agree; `validate` itself checks `cleaned(doc)` (a note at the length limit plus a variation selector fits), so the daemon's pre-check (`driver/settings.rs`) and the Settings screen's inline check agree with the save. C0, DEL, C1 and the two separators are refused with today's message. The daemon's `Saved` reply carries the cleaned doc. **A 9.0.6 pin changed:** `validate_refuses_control_characters_in_names_and_notes` no longer lists `a\u{202E}b` (a bidi override, now dropped on save); it gains DEL and U+0085 instead.
- **Wider hidden set.** `is_hidden_format` also drops U+034F, U+17B4–17B5, U+180B–180D, U+180F (U+180E was already in) and U+1D173–1D17A; U+2800 (a visible blank braille cell) is kept. `invisible_carriers_are_dropped` covers each, and pins U+E0080, U+E01F0, U+2800, U+034E, U+1D172 and U+1D17B as kept neighbours.
- **Tests.** `config` `validate_never_refuses_hidden_format_characters_and_cleaned_drops_them`; `save_tests.rs` `a_note_with_a_variation_selector_saves_cleaned` (a `config.toml` with comments and a `⚠️` note plus a `max_writers` change saves; the file has no U+FE0F, its note reads `⚠ careful`, both comments stay, the saved doc equals the read-back, no temporary file) and `a_control_character_in_a_note_still_refuses` (BEL refuses with the old message; file byte-identical). **Red first:** proto's test failed on the new ranges; config's tests did not compile (`cleaned` missing). **Mutants**, each red: the save not cleaning; `validate` checking the raw doc; the orchestrator model not cleaned; U+1D173–1D17A missing; U+2800 dropped. One equivalent survivor: `unsafe_char` still refusing hidden characters (unreachable, since `validate` runs on the cleaned doc).
- **Commands:** `cargo test -p anthrex-proto` 131, `-p anthrex-config` 124, `-p anthrex-tui --lib` 1136, `-p anthrex-daemon --lib -- run::delivery run::driver::settings settings live_config` 42, `-p anthrex --test run_e2e_settings --test run_e2e_settings_live` 9: all pass. fmt; `cargo +1.99 clippy -p anthrex-proto -p anthrex-config -p anthrex-daemon --all-targets -D warnings` (host and `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`); `cargo +1.98 check` of those and the TUI: clean.
- **Line counts:** `proto/src/safe_text.rs` 253, `config/src/settings/validate.rs` 232, `save.rs` 247, `save_tests.rs` 222, `tests.rs` 412.

### Task M9.2.7

- **What exists now.** `run/engine/delivery/` (pure): `mod.rs` (`DeliveryRequest { Deliver, Watch }` and `EventKind::Delivery`, the delivery pass, `host_done`, decision 11's error handling for the ops it emits, `may_complete`, `cancel_outcome`, `finish_refusal`, the `run deliver` and `run watch` handlers, and the helpers later tasks share: `stage_mut`, `stage_of`, `op_name`, `host_busy`, `emit`), `open.rs` (decision 19's `ready`, the empty-stage skip, tier 3 before opening, the push, the PR, its record). `engine/mod.rs` +4 (543: `pub mod delivery;`, the variant and its doc line, one arm), `dispatch.rs` +2 (591: the pass, before 9.1's idle tier 3 so `Deliver` takes the one tier-3 slot first), `results.rs` +2 (123: one row; the `use` list reflowed), `complete.rs` +17 (475), `full.rs` +2 (591), `reconcile/mod.rs` unchanged (M9.2.6 already added decision 10's row; tested here).
- **The ruling this task owns (9.0.6's actions in `pr` mode).** `rules::finish` asks `delivery::finish_refusal` first, so the request and the menu share one text: `run accept` is refused in `pr` mode in every state with decision 38's text; `run discard` with decision 39's text unless the run is `complete` (ruling R-1). `actions::relevant` never lists `Accept` for a `pr`-mode run and lists `Discard` only on a complete one; its effect line reads `discard: remove the run's local worktrees and anthrex/<id>/* branches, never a PR or a remote branch (…)`. A local run's menu is unchanged (pinned by `a_local_complete_run_still_lists_accept_and_discard`).
- **R-1's local discard** (with R-12 and task 4's ruling 1): `git::delete_branches`, which the existing `Discard` executor calls, also deletes `refs/anthrex/<run>/remote/*` and `refs/remotes/<remote>/anthrex/<run>/*` (the remote name is the first path segment), by compare-and-swap, locally. Nothing is pushed and no host is called: the engine's discard emits one `Discard` and no host op (`run_accept_and_discard_are_refused_in_pr_mode`), and `delete_branches_also_removes_the_runs_private_remote_refs_but_never_the_remotes` (real git, a local bare remote) shows the remote's stage branch untouched and another run's, `origin/main`'s and the salvage refs kept. The end-to-end check that `FakeHost`'s `calls.jsonl` gains nothing for a discard needs M9.2.12's executor; it belongs to M9.2.16's `e2e_pr_run_accept_and_discard_are_refused`.
- **The carried ruling (idempotent opening across a restart).** Nothing is stored between the push and the PR but the pushed head: each pass re-derives what is due from the run, so a host op that reconcile drops as `NotStarted` is emitted again once the run is resumed (`Push`, then `UpToDate`, then `OpenPr`, which `GhHost::open_pr` answers with the PR it made, `existed: true`). `opening_survives_restarts_without_a_second_pr` executes the engine's ops against a real `FakeHost` (a local bare remote; no network, no `gh` process) with a restart between the push and its record and another between `gh pr create` and its record: one PR, `gh pr create` ran once, `forbidden.jsonl` empty.
- **Deviations** (none changes a design decision; each is forced or a reading the brief left open):
  1. **Files beyond the list.** The tests are three files (`delivery_open.rs` 595, `delivery_requests.rs` 402, `delivery_open_host.rs` 198; AGENTS.md rule 8). `run/delivery/mod.rs` gains two `StageDelivery` fields (deviation 2) and `run/delivery/tests.rs`'s round-trip literal sets them. `driver/guard.rs` +1 (compile-forced: the panic guard's `reply_of` answers a `Delivery` request). `engine/actions/{mod,rules,effects}.rs` (the ruling above). `git/salvage.rs` +45 and `tests/run_git_finish.rs` (R-1/R-12). `engine/tests/full.rs`: 9.1's pin `full_request_does_nothing_for_an_untiered_profile` asserted exactly what decision 19 tells this task to change; it became `full_request_runs_only_check_for_an_untiered_profile_before_delivery` (`Idle` and `Completion` still do nothing and change nothing; `Deliver` starts one job).
  2. **`StageDelivery.pushed` and `.retry_at`** (`#[serde(default)]` through the struct's): the head the opening push sent, consumed into `PrRecord.pushed_head` (the stage head may move between the push and the PR's record; `the_pr_records_the_head_its_push_sent`), and when a failed opening op may be retried.
  3. **`pub mod delivery;`** in `engine/mod.rs`: `EventKind` is public and M9.2.12's driver builds `DeliveryRequest`. `host_done` takes the `OpResult` so `results.rs`'s row stays one line.
  4. **An empty stage is one that merged nothing** (every task cancelled or reported), the brief's "every task cancelled" read so a research-only stage, which also has no commits, is skipped too. It is skipped once decision 19's first four conditions hold.
  5. **Unverified**: a profile with no `check` (tiered or not) opens without tier 3; the body already says so (M9.2.6). Tier 3 is requested only when the head is neither green nor red; a red head waits for 9.1's bisect or a fix task to move it.
  6. **The untiered branch** (`full::request`): `why == Deliver && check.is_some()` lets an untiered profile through; `start` builds the same `Tier { tier: 3 }` job, and the executor already runs an untiered spec's `check` as written (no cache, no retry, `driver/tier.rs`). No bisect: `full::red` gives an untiered red the "no single culprit" end with the reason `the profile is untiered, so nothing is bisected` (its attention line and wake note are 9.1's).
  7. **Completion in `pr` mode** (ruling R-8): `complete_pass` adds `delivery::may_complete` (every stage PR merged or closed, skipped stages done, bypassed for a cancelled run) and skips `full::holds_completion`; `refs_verified` completes a `pr`-mode run at once on `RefsOk`, so neither 9.1's `full::completion` nor M8a's final check runs (an untiered profile's tier 3 is its `check`, decision 19). `propagate::holds_completion` still applies.
  8. **Push outcomes at opening.** `Rejected` halts with TT's `remote stage branch was rewritten by someone else` and the log line `stage <n>: <remote> refused to fast-forward anthrex/<run>/stage-<n> to <sha7> (<reason>)` (the Messages line names a PR and the remote's commit, neither of which exists before the PR). **`Refused` (R-11) halts the run, retryable by `run resume`, with R-11's text as the reason**, not a stage hold: no stage-hold state, attention line or resume path exists yet, and holding by retrying would push every second. For the controller: if a stage hold is wanted, it is M9.2.8's (which owns attention lines and failures).
  9. **Decision 11 for the opening ops**: `Forbidden` halts with `anthrex refused its own host command: <argv>` (the `anthrex never runs: ` prefix stripped); any other error logs `stage <n>: <op> failed: <error, first 200 characters>`, counts in `RunDelivery.failures["<n>/<op>"]` (reset by the next success of that op) and sets `retry_at` one poll interval later (`max(poll_base_secs, poll_secs, 1)`). The attention line after 5 failures, rate limits and auth are M9.2.8's.
  10. **Texts the Messages table leaves open** (invented): `run deliver` on a stage whose PR is merged `stage <n>'s PR is already merged: <url>`, closed `stage <n>'s PR was closed without merging: <url>`, skipped `stage <n> has no changes, so it opens no pull request`; on a run that is not running `run <id> is <state>` (both requests on a complete or ended run), on a cancelled one `run <id> was cancelled`, an unknown run `unknown run <id>`. The accepted text is the table's whenever the stage is ready (also when tier 3 is already running or green). Log lines: `stage <n>: pushing <sha7> to anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>`, `stage <n>: PR #<k> opened: <url>`, `stage <n>: PR #<k> already existed; adopted: <url>`, `stage <n>: skipped (no changes)`. Decision 37's closed-below check comes before "stage <n−1>'s PR is not open", so a closed lower PR is named as such.
  11. **The cancel outcome is pluralised**: `cancelled; 1 pull request left open: #7` (the table's plural for two or more, `cancelled` with none). It is the run's `outcome` and a log line. `run cancel` sets `watching = false`; the pass emits nothing for a cancelled run, and a push answered after the cancel opens no PR.
  12. `ready_at` is set when the stage first passes decision 19's first four conditions; a new PR's `next_poll_at` is one poll interval after it opened (M9.2.8 owns polling).

### Task M9.2.8

- **What exists now.** `run/engine/delivery/watch.rs` (the per-stage order each pass: an adopt, else a push, else a view when due; decision 23's interval and cap; the push of an open PR's moved stage head, decision 28; the adopt's fetch and its answers, decision 24; decision 11's rate limit, lost login and "keeps failing" line; ruling R-11's per-stage hold; `stage_busy`, `stage_paused`, `held`, `release`, `attention`) and `run/engine/delivery/view.rs` (a view processed against the watermark in the one step that answers it). `engine/delivery/mod.rs` routes `PrViewed`, an open PR's `Pushed` and an adopt's `Fetched`, and its `failed` dispatches decision 11's cases. 9.1's `stages::ready_in_stage` (+4: `stage_paused`), `merge::start_merge` (+4: `stage_busy` for a candidate) and `propagate::start` (+1: `stage_busy` for a propagate into the stage) each gained one condition. `snapshot::attention` gains one line (the delivery lines).
- **The view** (decision 23, rulings R-2 and R-5). In the step that answers a `ViewPr`: the PR's `state`, `base` (the host's, review m3), `merged_at`/`merge_commit` and `checks` (`PrRecord.checks`, task 6's ruling: name one-lined and cut to 100 characters, `red`/`green`/`pending`, unknown pending) are the view's; every item above the watermark becomes a `ThreadRecord` in state `new`: a conversation comment `c<id>`, a `CHANGES_REQUESTED` review's body `r<id>` (other reviews only move the watermark), a review thread `t<id of its first comment>`, which a later comment in it updates (`last_comment_id`, `text`), never duplicates; the three watermark ids move to the highest seen; a red CI run on the pushed head with every check completed is recorded once per set of failing check names (`Watermark.ci[head] = CiSeen { failing (sorted), at }`, at most `CI_RECORDS_MAX` heads); `next_poll_at` is `now + poll_base × 2^k` (open) or `now + cap` (merged, closed), `k` reset by any change (state, head, mergeable, checks, a new id). Log lines carry ids and check names only: `stage <n> (PR #<pr>): new threads <keys>`, `stage <n> (PR #<pr>): CI red at <sha7>: <checks>`.
- **Deviations and readings** (none changes a design decision):
  1. **What a view's records lead to is later tasks'.** Task 8 records threads and red CI runs against the watermark (so "processed once" is real and survives a restart, `restore_replays_no_event_twice`), but decides nothing from them: no filtering by author, bot, marker or resolution (decision 29, M9.2.10 turns a record `ignored` with its reason), no `CiRecord` or `FailedLogs` (decision 27, M9.2.9 starts at the `CiSeen` insert in `view.rs`), no landing (decision 35, M9.2.11; a view records `Merged`/`Closed` and polls at the cap). Until M9.2.10, a bot's comment is a `new` thread in the snapshot's counts; nothing reaches an agent.
  2. **Model fields** (each `#[serde(default)]`, not in Interfaces): `StageDelivery.remote_head` (the head a view saw that is not the pushed one: an adopt is due; a view, not the pass, decides it, so the stale `Watermark.head` after anthrex's own push never looks foreign), `StageDelivery.held` (ruling R-11's reason), `RunDelivery.alerts` (attention lines by key: `auth`, and a failure key once it reaches 5; removed when what they report clears). `run/delivery/tests.rs`'s round-trip literal sets all three.
  3. **Adoption.** `Fetch { stage: Some(n), branch: anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>, into: refs/anthrex/<run>/remote/stage-<n>, adopt: { local_ref: run.stage_branch(n) (integration for a Single run), expected_local: the stage head, also_integration: Multi and n is the highest } }`. It waits for the stage's merge queue (open.rs's `queue_busy`, now `pub(super)`), a paused or held stage, and a failed op's `retry_at`; while it is out, `start_merge` starts no candidate into the stage and `propagate::start` skips a propagate into it (the next due one is taken). `Adopted` with a new sha: `set_stage_head` (stage n+1 due its propagate), then **ruling R-9** through `stages::forget_line`, the body of 9.1's `moved_line` split out without its `rebaselined to` log line (the commit is unrecorded on the line, so `merges` are cleared and it becomes `floor`; a bisect in flight ends `rebaselined`; a tier-3 result off the line is forgotten), `pushed_head = sha`, log `stage <n> (PR #<pr>): adopted <sha7> from <branch>`, no push. `NotDescendant { remote }` halts with TT's text and the Messages log line; **a `remote` equal to `pushed_head` is not a rewrite** (the view lagged behind the branch, which holds what anthrex pushed; the adopt is dropped and a push of the newer local head follows). `LocalMoved` logs and adopts again from the new head; `Missing` logs `… is gone from the remote`.
  4. **A rewrite halt is retryable** (decision 24: "`run resume` polls again and halts again"): `halt_retryable`, `remote_head` cleared and the PR's view made due, so the resumed run views first (a PR closed meanwhile is seen) and, still foreign, fetches and halts again. The same for an update push answered `Rejected`; task 7's opening `Rejected` halt stays as it was (not retryable, tested there).
  5. **Both sides moved.** A user's commit on the remote while a merged fix waits locally to be pushed: the adopt (it goes first) fetches a remote that does not descend from the local head, so the run halts as rewritten (decision 24's rule, applied as written). For the controller: nothing was rewritten; a merge of the two would be the friendlier answer, but it is a new behaviour, not this task's.
  6. **Ruling R-11's hold**: `stage <n> is held: <reason>; anthrex run resume <run> pushes it again` (an attention line, a log line and a wake note; `<reason>` is `PushOutcome::Refused`'s `the remote refused the push of anthrex/<run>/stage-<n>: <git's reason>`). It replaces task 7's halt for the opening push too. A held stage pushes nothing (opening or update) and still polls; `run resume` of a running run releases every held stage (`actions::rules::resume` accepts it, `restore::resume` answers `run <id>: held pushes retry (stage <n>, …)`, beside 9.1's `tier 3 retries` in one reply when both); a paused or halted run's resume releases them too; a successful push of the stage clears it.
  7. **Decision 11's cases.** A `RateLimited` answer doubles `poll_base_secs` (at most 3 600, at least `poll_secs`) and is not counted as a failure; any successful host call restores `poll_secs`. `Auth` sets the `auth` alert `gh is no longer logged in to <host>; run gh auth login` until any success. Any other error counts in `failures` and, at 5, sets `PR #<pr>: <op> keeps failing: <error, 200 characters>` (`stage <n>: …` before a PR exists, `the run: …` for a run-level op), cleared with the count by the op's next success. A failed view backs off one step (`k + 1`; a rate-limited one does not, its base already doubled) and is retried on that interval; other ops keep task 7's `retry_at`.
  8. **Decision 28's push** waits for: the PR open, the stage not paused or held, no adopt due, no host op of the stage in flight, the queue quiet (no queued task of the stage, no due propagate or base sync into it, no candidate or propagate in flight for it) and `retry_at`. Answered `Pushed`/`UpToDate`: `pushed_head`, `held` cleared, `k = 0` and the view due within one base interval (CI starts on the new head), log `stage <n> (PR #<pr>): pushed <sha7>`.
  9. **Carried minors from task 7's review.** m1: `open::pushed` re-checks `ready` before `OpenPr` (`opening_waits_when_the_stage_is_no_longer_ready_after_its_push`). m3: the view overwrites `PrRecord.base`. m4: `rules::finish` gives an ended run's state text first, and a discardable cancelled halted run (review m2) is not refused for its delivery (`delivery_refusals_follow_the_state_checks`); `run accept` keeps decision 38's text in every other state. m5: the doc note in `git/salvage.rs::delete_remote_refs`.
  10. **Files.** The view's processing is its own `view.rs` (`watch.rs` passed 600 after `cargo fmt`); the tests are two files, `delivery_watch.rs` (polling, the watermark, restore, `run watch`, decision 11) and `delivery_watch_adopt.rs` (adoption, the queue, pushes, the hold, the pause, m1). `delivery_open.rs` (595) and `dispatch.rs` (591) are untouched, so no move commit was needed. `opened` uses `watch::base_secs` for the first view (it read `poll_base_secs`, 0 on a run built before M9.2.12's driver sets it).
- **Tests first** (19 new, all engine tests driven by scripted `OpResult::Host` answers; no host runs): `delivery_watch.rs` `poll_is_due_on_the_interval_and_backs_off_when_unchanged` (1, 2, 4, 8, 8, and a cap below the base is the base), `a_change_resets_the_backoff` (a comment, mergeability, a pending check, its conclusion), `closed_and_merged_prs_are_polled_at_the_cap` (and a reopen, and the host's base), `rate_limit_doubles_the_interval_until_a_success` (and the hour's cap), `watermark_processes_each_comment_and_check_once`, `restore_replays_no_event_twice` (the run through `run.json`'s serde, `Restore`, `run resume`, the same view: no task, wake note, reply, thread or log line), `watch_off_emits_no_view_and_on_polls_at_once` (a merged fix is still pushed), `auth_lost_is_an_attention_line_and_polling_continues`, `five_failures_raise_an_attention_line`; `delivery_watch_adopt.rs` `user_commit_is_adopted_and_propagated` (with R-9's floor), `rewritten_remote_halts_with_the_exact_text` (twice, through `run resume`), `a_stale_view_is_not_a_rewrite`, `adopt_waits_for_the_stages_queue_and_the_queue_waits_for_the_adopt`, `a_propagate_waits_for_an_adopt_of_its_stage` (and `also_integration` for the highest stage), `push_waits_for_the_stages_queue_then_pushes_the_new_head`, `a_refused_push_holds_its_stage_and_run_resume_pushes_again`, `a_paused_stage_starts_no_task`, `opening_waits_when_the_stage_is_no_longer_ready_after_its_push`; `delivery_requests.rs` `delivery_refusals_follow_the_state_checks`, and task 7's `a_rejected_push_halts_and_a_forbidden_command_halts` became `…_a_refused_one_holds_…` (ruling R-11 replaces the halt it pinned).
- **Red.** Against a stub `watch.rs` (every function a no-op or `false`, the routing in place, a refused push still halting): 21 of the 40 `engine::tests::delivery` tests failed: the 19 new ones, the renamed refusal test, and task 7's `a_host_error_is_retried_after_the_poll_interval` (the stub's success did not reset the count). **Mutants**, 26, each applied to a copy, run against its tests and restored: no doubling; the cap ignoring the base; closed not at the cap; the rate limit a no-op; a success keeping the doubled base; the comment watermark not advanced; `CiSeen` ignored; pending checks ignored; the adopt ignoring the queue; `stage_busy` false; the propagate or the candidate ignoring it; no floor; no stale guard; the push ignoring the queue; a refused push halting; `stage_paused` false; no `ready` re-check; the old `finish` order; `run watch --off` ignored; the auth line a no-op; the threshold at 4; a failed view not backing off; no adopt due; the base not overwritten; `run resume` not releasing. Three survived the first run (the cap, pending checks, the base), so their tests were sharpened (a base of 3 over a cap of 0; a first view with `lint` still running; a base of `release`); all 26 are red.
